    if(!b||!v){r[12]=o;r[13]=o;return;}

    const f=R.find(r=>r[0]===k), rem=1-w/100;
    R.push([k,f[1],'Dağılımı Raporlanmayan Kısım','Fonun portföy dağılımı raporlanmadığı için kalan tutar','Dağılım Raporlanmadı','Dağılım Raporlanmadı',+(100-w).toFixed(2),0,'Dağılımı Raporlanmayan Kısım',0,'',R.length,b[1]*rem,b[0]*rem]);
  });
})();

/* ---------- Yardımcılar ---------- */
const nf = (v,d=0)=>v.toLocaleString('tr-TR',{minimumFractionDigits:d,maximumFractionDigits:d});
const fmtTL = v=>{const a=Math.abs(v);
  if(a>=1e9) return nf(v/1e9,2)+' Mr ₺';
  if(a>=1e6) return nf(v/1e6,2)+' Mn ₺';
  return nf(v,0)+' ₺';};
const fmtShort = v=>{const a=Math.abs(v); return a>=1e9?nf(v/1e9,1)+' Mr':a>=1e6?nf(v/1e6,0)+' Mn':nf(v/1e3,0)+' B';};
const fmtFull = v=>nf(v,0)+' ₺';
// 1.000 Mr ve üzeri toplamlar tam sayıya yuvarlanır (ör. 11.560,66 → 11.561 Mr ₺)
const fmtTLr = v=>Math.abs(v)>=1e12 ? nf(v/1e9,0)+' Mr ₺' : fmtTL(v);
const fmtW = w=>nf(w,2)+'%';
const esc = s=>String(s==null?'':s).replace(/[&<>"']/g,c=>({'&':'&amp;','<':'&lt;','>':'&gt;','"':'&quot;',"'":'&#39;'}[c]));
const $ = id=>document.getElementById(id);
const lc = s=>String(s==null?'':s).toLocaleLowerCase('tr');
const num = s=>{if(s===''||s==null)return null;const v=parseFloat(String(s).replace(',','.'));return isNaN(v)?null:v;};
const isTem = r=>r[4].indexOf('Ters Repo')===0;
const ensOf = r=>{const a=(r[3]||'').split(' · ')[0].trim();return a||'—';};
const distinct = (rows,i)=>new Set(rows.map(r=>r[i])).size;
const money = v=>`<span class="${v<0?'neg':''}" title="${fmtFull(v)}">${fmtTL(v)}</span>`;

/* ---------- Referans tarih, kalan gün ---------- */
let REF=(()=>{const n=new Date();return new Date(n.getFullYear(),n.getMonth(),n.getDate());})();
function endDate(vk){const y=Math.floor(vk/10000),m=Math.floor(vk/100)%100,d=vk%100;return d?new Date(y,m-1,d):new Date(y,m,0);}
function kalanKey(vk){return vk?Math.round((endDate(vk)-REF)/864e5):null;}
const kalan = r=>kalanKey(r[9]);
function isoDate(d){return d.getFullYear()+'-'+String(d.getMonth()+1).padStart(2,'0')+'-'+String(d.getDate()).padStart(2,'0');}

/* ---------- Kurum tipi (isimden tahmin) ---------- */
function guessTip(k){const s=lc(k);
  if(s.indexOf('devlet tahvili')===0||s.indexOf('hazine')===0) return 'Hazine';
  if(/varlık kiralama/.test(s)) return 'Varlık kiralama';
  if(/banka|bank\b/.test(s)) return 'Banka';
  if(/faktoring|finansman|finansal kiralama|leasing|kiralama|ipotek/.test(s)) return 'Finans / faktoring';
  if(/yatırım|menkul|securities|capital/.test(s)) return 'Aracı kurum';
  return 'Diğer ihraççı';}
const kTip=(()=>{const hasV=new Map(),tur=new Map();
  R.forEach(r=>{if(r[9])hasV.set(r[8],1);if(!tur.has(r[8]))tur.set(r[8],r[4]);});
  const out=new Map(); tur.forEach((t,k)=>out.set(k,hasV.get(k)?guessTip(k):t)); return out;})();

const funds = (()=>{const m=new Map();R.forEach(r=>{if(!m.has(r[0]))m.set(r[0],r[1])});return [...m].sort((a,b)=>a[0].localeCompare(b[0],'tr'))})();

const colorOf = {4:new Map(), 5:new Map()};
[4,5].forEach(g=>{
  const t=new Map(); R.forEach(r=>t.set(r[g],(t.get(r[g])||0)+Math.abs(r[7])));
  [...t].sort((a,b)=>b[1]-a[1]).forEach(([k],i)=>colorOf[g].set(k,PALETTE[i%PALETTE.length]));
});

function filtered(){ return state.funds.size ? R.filter(r=>state.funds.has(r[0])) : R; }
function scopeRows(){ let rows=filtered(); if(!state.incl) rows=rows.filter(r=>!isTem(r)); return rows; }

/* ---------- Genel tablo motoru: sıralama (başlığa tıkla) + isteğe bağlı filtre ---------- */
const GT={};
function gtReg(id,cfg){ GT[id]={cfg,st:{sort:null,f:{},showF:false}}; }
function gtReset(){ [...arguments].forEach(i=>{ if(GT[i]) GT[i].st={sort:null,f:{},showF:false}; }); }
function gtActive(id){let n=0;Object.values(GT[id].st.f).forEach(o=>Object.values(o).forEach(v=>{if(String(v||'').trim())n++;}));return n;}
function gtPass(c,st,r,skipK){
  return c.cols.every(col=>{
    if(col.k===skipK) return true;
    const f=st.f[col.k]; if(!f) return true;
    const val=col.get(r);
    if(col.type==='n'){
      const mn=num(f.min), mx=num(f.max); if(mn===null&&mx===null) return true;
      if(val==null||isNaN(val)) return false;
      const x=val/(col.div||1); return (mn===null||x>=mn)&&(mx===null||x<=mx);
    }
    if(col.choice){ if(col.multi){ const m=f.m; if(!m||!m.length) return true; return col.vals?col.vals(r).some(v=>m.includes(String(v))):m.includes(String(val)); } if(!f.s) return true; return String(val)===f.s; }
    const q=lc(f.t).trim(); if(!q) return true; return lc(val).includes(q);
  });
}
// Seçmeli sütun: seçenekler, diğer sütunlardaki filtrelere uyan satırlardan türetilir (yanında fon/kayıt sayısı)
function gtChoiceData(c,st,col){
  const f=st.f[col.k]||{};
  if(col.opts) return col.opts.map(o=>({v:o,t:o}));
  const cnt=new Map();
  c.rows.forEach(r=>{ if(!gtPass(c,st,r,col.k)) return; if(col.vals){ col.vals(r).forEach(v=>cnt.set(v,(cnt.get(v)||0)+1)); return; } const v=col.get(r); if(v!==''&&v!=null) cnt.set(v,(cnt.get(v)||0)+1); });
  if(f.s&&!cnt.has(f.s)) cnt.set(f.s,0);   // seçili değer her zaman listede kalır
  (f.m||[]).forEach(v=>{ if(!cnt.has(v)) cnt.set(v,0); });
  return [...cnt.keys()].sort((a,b)=>String(a).localeCompare(String(b),'tr')).map(o=>({v:o,t:(col.lab?col.lab(o):o)+' ('+nf(cnt.get(o))+')'}));
}
function gtChoiceOptions(c,st,col){
  const f=st.f[col.k]||{};
  return `<option value="">Tümü</option>`+gtChoiceData(c,st,col).map(o=>`<option value="${esc(o.v)}" ${f.s===o.v?'selected':''}>${esc(o.t)}</option>`).join('');
}
const FUNNEL='<svg viewBox="0 0 16 16" width="12" height="12" aria-hidden="true"><path d="M1.5 2.5h13l-5 6v4.5l-3 1.5V8.5z" fill="currentColor"/></svg>';
// Sütun başlığındaki filtre düğmesinin açtığı mini pencere (sayfa üstünde sabit konumlu, tablo kaydırmasından etkilenmez)
let GP=null;
const TOUCH=(()=>{try{return matchMedia('(pointer:coarse)').matches||innerWidth<=760}catch(e){return false}})();
function gtPopClose(){ const p=$('gtPop'); if(p) p.style.display='none'; GP=null; }
function gtPopList(){
  const g=GT[GP.id], c=g.cfg(), col=c.cols.find(x=>x.k===GP.k), st=g.st, f=st.f[col.k]||{}, q=lc(GP.q).trim();
  const items=gtChoiceData(c,st,col).filter(o=>!q||lc(o.t).includes(q));
  if(col.multi){ const m=f.m||[]; return items.map(o=>`<label class="gtpi mi"><input type="checkbox" data-gtpm="${esc(o.v)}" ${m.includes(String(o.v))?'checked':''}> <span>${esc(o.t)}</span></label>`).join('')||'<div class="empty" style="padding:10px">Sonuç yok</div>'; }
  return `<div class="gtpi ${!f.s?'sel':''}" data-gtpv="">Tümü</div>`+(items.map(o=>`<div class="gtpi ${f.s===o.v?'sel':''}" data-gtpv="${esc(o.v)}">${esc(o.t)}</div>`).join('')||'<div class="empty" style="padding:10px">Sonuç yok</div>');
}
function gtPopToggle(btn){
  const [id,k]=btn.dataset.gtpop.split('|');
  if(GP&&GP.id===id&&GP.k===k){gtPopClose();return}
  GP={id,k,q:'',t:Date.now()};
  const g=GT[id], c=g.cfg(), col=c.cols.find(x=>x.k===k), many=gtChoiceData(c,g.st,col).length>(col.multi?6:8), nsel=((g.st.f[k]||{}).m||[]).length;
  const p=$('gtPop');
  p.innerHTML=`<div class="gtph">${esc(col.label)}${col.multi?` <span id="gtPopCnt" class="gtcnt">${nsel?nsel+' seçili':''}</span>`:''}</div>${col.multi?'<div class="gtpa"><button class="ghost" data-gtpall="1">Tümünü Seç</button><button class="ghost" data-gtpnone="1">Temizle</button></div>':''}${many?'<input type="search" id="gtPopQ" placeholder="Listede arama…">':''}<div class="gtpl" id="gtPopList">${gtPopList()}</div>`;
  p.style.display='block';
  const r=btn.getBoundingClientRect(), w=p.offsetWidth, h=p.offsetHeight;
  let left=Math.min(Math.max(8,r.left),innerWidth-w-8), top=r.bottom+6; if(top+h>innerHeight-8) top=Math.max(8,r.top-h-6);
  p.style.left=left+'px'; p.style.top=top+'px';
  const qi=$('gtPopQ'); if(qi&&!TOUCH) qi.focus();
}
function gtPopApply(v){ const id=GP.id,k=GP.k,st=GT[id].st; st.f[k]=st.f[k]||{}; st.f[k].s=v; gtPopClose(); gtBody(id); }
function gtRender(id){
  const g=GT[id], el=$('gt_'+id); if(!g||!el) return;
  const c=g.cfg(), st=g.st;
  const head=c.cols.map(col=>{
    const s=st.sort&&st.sort.k===col.k, arr=s?(st.sort.dir==='asc'?'▲':'▼'):'↕';
    const fb=col.choice?` <button class="fbtn ${(st.f[col.k]&&(st.f[col.k].s||(st.f[col.k].m&&st.f[col.k].m.length)))?'on':''}" id="gtfb_${id}_${col.k}" data-gtpop="${id}|${col.k}" title="Bu sütunda filtre seç">${FUNNEL}</button>`:'';
    return `<th class="${col.type==='n'?'r':''} sortable" data-gts="${id}|${col.k}" title="Sıralamak için tıklanabilir">${esc(col.label)} <span class="arr ${s?'on':''}">${arr}</span>${fb}</th>`;
  }).join('');
  let fil='';
  if(st.showF){
    fil='<tr class="fr">'+c.cols.map(col=>{
      const f=st.f[col.k]||{};
      if(col.type==='n'){
        const u=col.unit?` ${col.unit}`:'';
        return `<th class="fl r"><div class="pair"><input type="text" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="min${u}" data-gtf="${id}|${col.k}|min" value="${esc(f.min||'')}"><input type="text" inputmode="decimal" placeholder="max${u}" data-gtf="${id}|${col.k}|max" value="${esc(f.max||'')}"></div></th>`;
      }
      if(col.choice&&col.multi){ const n=(f.m||[]).length; return `<th class="fl"><button class="chsel mbtn" id="gts_${id}_${col.k}" data-gtpop="${id}|${col.k}">${n?n+' seçili':'Tümü'}</button></th>`; }
      if(col.choice){
        return `<th class="fl"><select class="chsel" id="gts_${id}_${col.k}" data-gtf="${id}|${col.k}|s" title="Filtre seçimi">${gtChoiceOptions(c,st,col)}</select></th>`;
      }
      return `<th class="fl"><input type="text" placeholder="filtre…" data-gtf="${id}|${col.k}|t" value="${esc(f.t||'')}"></th>`;
    }).join('')+'</tr>';
  }
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="gtbar"><span class="gray" id="gtc_${id}"></span><span class="sp"></span><button class="ghost" id="gtx_${id}" data-gtc="${id}" style="display:none">Filtreleri temizle</button><button class="ghost ${st.showF?'on':''}" data-gtt="${id}">🔍 Filtrele<span class="badge" id="gtn_${id}" style="display:none"></span></button></div>
    <div class="scroll gts ${st.showF?'withf':''}" style="max-height:${c.maxH||520}px"><table><thead><tr>${head}</tr>${fil}</thead><tbody id="gtb_${id}"></tbody><tfoot id="gtf_${id}"></tfoot></table></div>`;
  gtBody(id);
}
function gtBody(id){
  const g=GT[id]; if(!g||!$('gtb_'+id)) return;
  const c=g.cfg(), st=g.st;
  let rows=c.rows.filter(r=>gtPass(c,st,r));
  const sk=st.sort||c.defSort;
  if(sk){
    const col=c.cols.find(x=>x.k===sk.k);
    if(col){ const key=col.sv||col.get, d=sk.dir==='asc'?1:-1;
      rows=rows.slice().sort((a,b)=>{
        const x=key(a), y=key(b), nx=(x==null||x===''), ny=(y==null||y==='');
        if(nx&&ny) return 0; if(nx) return 1; if(ny) return -1;
        return (typeof x==='number'&&typeof y==='number'? x-y : String(x).localeCompare(String(y),'tr'))*d;
      });
    }
  }
  $('gtb_'+id).innerHTML = rows.length
    ? rows.map(r=>`<tr class="${c.rowAttr?'clickable ':''}${c.rowCls?c.rowCls(r):''}" ${c.rowAttr?c.rowAttr(r):''}>${c.cols.map(col=>`<td class="${col.cls!==undefined?col.cls:(col.type==='n'?'r':'')}">${col.html?col.html(r):esc(col.get(r))}</td>`).join('')}</tr>`).join('')
    : `<tr><td colspan="${c.cols.length}" class="empty">Kayıt yok</td></tr>`;
  $('gtf_'+id).innerHTML = (c.footer&&rows.length)?c.footer(rows):'';
  const n=gtActive(id), cnt=$('gtc_'+id), bd=$('gtn_'+id), cx=$('gtx_'+id);
  if(cnt) cnt.textContent = nf(rows.length)+(rows.length!==c.rows.length?` / ${nf(c.rows.length)}`:'')+' kayıt';
  if(bd){bd.textContent=n;bd.style.display=n?'':'none';}
  if(cx) cx.style.display=n?'':'none';
  c.cols.forEach(col=>{ if(!col.choice||col.opts) return; const el=$('gts_'+id+'_'+col.k); if(!el) return; if(col.multi){ const n=((st.f[col.k]||{}).m||[]).length; el.textContent=n?n+' seçili':'Tümü'; return; } el.innerHTML=gtChoiceOptions(c,st,col); el.value=(st.f[col.k]&&st.f[col.k].s)||''; });
  c.cols.forEach(col=>{ if(!col.choice) return; const bt=$('gtfb_'+id+'_'+col.k); if(bt) bt.classList.toggle('on',!!(st.f[col.k]&&(st.f[col.k].s||(st.f[col.k].m&&st.f[col.k].m.length)))); });
  if(c.after) c.after(rows);
}
function gtSort(id,k){
  const st=GT[id].st;
  if(!st.sort||st.sort.k!==k) st.sort={k,dir:'asc'};
  else if(st.sort.dir==='asc') st.sort={k,dir:'desc'};
  else st.sort=null;
  gtRender(id);
}
const gtBox = id=>`<div id="gt_${id}"></div>`;

/* ---------- Toplulaştırma ---------- */
function aggregate(rows){
  const g=state.group, m=new Map(), fs=new Set();
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    fs.add(r[0]);
    let o=m.get(r[g]); if(!o){o={key:r[g],n:0,w:0,v:0,mk:new Set(),f:new Set()};m.set(r[g],o)}
    o.n++; o.w+=r[6]; o.v+=r[7]; o.mk.add(r[2]); o.f.add(r[0]);
  });
  const nF=Math.max(1,fs.size);
  const list=[...m.values()].map(o=>({...o, wAvg:o.w/nF}));
  const mv=o=>state.metric==='v'?o.v:o.wAvg;
  list.sort((a,b)=>mv(b)-mv(a));
  return {list,nF,mv};
}

/* ================= SAYFA 1: FON PORTFÖY ================= */
gtReg('sum',()=>{
  const rows=filtered(), {list,nF,mv}=aggregate(rows);
  const pos=list.reduce((s,o)=>s+Math.max(0,mv(o)),0)||1;
  return {maxH:600, rows:list, defSort:{k:'val',dir:'desc'}, rowAttr:o=>`data-key="${esc(o.key)}"`, rowCls:o=>state.sel===o.key?'sel':'',
    cols:[
      {k:'key',label:state.group===4?'Varlık Türü':'Ana Sınıf',type:'t',get:o=>o.key,
        html:o=>{const c=colorOf[state.group].get(o.key),share=Math.max(0,mv(o))/pos*100;return `<span class="dot" style="background:${c}"></span><b>${esc(o.key)}</b><div class="bar"><i style="width:${share}%;background:${c}"></i></div>`;}},
      {k:'n',label:'Adet',type:'n',get:o=>o.n,html:o=>nf(o.n)},
      {k:'mk',label:'Menkul Kıymet',type:'n',get:o=>o.mk.size,html:o=>nf(o.mk.size)},
      {k:'val',label:'Tahmini Değer',type:'n',get:o=>o.v,div:1e6,unit:'Mn',html:o=>money(o.v)}
    ],
    footer:rs=>{const tv=rs.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.v,0),tn=rs.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.n,0);
      return `<tr><td><b>Toplam</b></td><td class="r"><b>${nf(tn)}</b></td><td></td><td class="r"><b>${money(tv)}</b></td></tr>`;}
  };
});

gtReg('tasf50',()=>{
  const rows=FX.filter(f=>f.d50);
  return {maxH:420,rows,defSort:{k:'tut1',dir:'desc'},rowAttr:f=>`data-tasfsel="${esc(f.kod)}"`,rowCls:f=>state.funds.has(f.kod)?'sel':'',
    cols:[
      {k:'kod',label:'Fon Kodu',type:'t',get:f=>f.kod,html:f=>`<b>${esc(f.kod)}</b>`},
      {k:'ad',label:'Fon Adı',type:'t',get:f=>f.ad,cls:'desc'},
      {k:'yon',label:'Yönetim Şirketi',type:'t',choice:true,get:f=>f.yon},
      {k:'tut1',label:'Fon Büyüklüğü',type:'n',get:f=>f.tut1,div:1e6,unit:'Mn',html:f=>money(f.tut1)}
    ],
    footer:rs=>{const a=rs.reduce((s,f)=>s+f.tut1,0);
      return `<tr><td><b>${nf(rs.length)} fon</b></td><td></td><td></td><td class="r"><b>${money(a)}</b></td></tr>`;}
  };
});
gtReg('kurum',()=>{
  const rows=filtered();
  let sc=rows;
  if(state.sel) sc=sc.filter(r=>r[state.group]===state.sel);
  if(!state.incl) sc=sc.filter(r=>!isTem(r));
  const nF=Math.max(1,distinct(rows,0));
  const m=new Map();
  sc.forEach(r=>{
    let o=m.get(r[8]); if(!o){o={k:r[8],n:0,w:0,v:0,f:new Set(),vd:new Set()};m.set(r[8],o)}
    o.n++;o.w+=r[6];o.v+=r[7];o.f.add(r[0]); if(r[10])o.vd.add(r[10]);
  });
  return {maxH:480, rows:[...m.values()], defSort:{k:'v',dir:'desc'}, rowAttr:o=>`data-kurum="${esc(o.k)}"`,
    cols:[
      {k:'k',label:'Kurum',type:'t',get:o=>o.k,html:o=>`<b>${esc(o.k)}</b>`},
      {k:'n',label:'Kalem',type:'n',get:o=>o.n,html:o=>nf(o.n)},
      {k:'f',label:'Fon',type:'n',get:o=>o.f.size,html:o=>nf(o.f.size)},
      {k:'vd',label:'Vade',type:'n',get:o=>o.vd.size,html:o=>o.vd.size?nf(o.vd.size):'—'},
      {k:'v',label:'Değer',type:'n',get:o=>o.v,div:1e6,unit:'Mn',html:o=>money(o.v)}
    ]
  };
});

function detailScope(){ return filtered().filter(r=>r[state.group]===state.sel); }
gtReg('detail',()=>{
  const scope=state.sel?detailScope():[];
  const q=lc(state.dq).trim();
  const items=q?scope.filter(r=>lc(r[2]+' '+(r[3]||'')+' '+r[0]+' '+r[1]+' '+r[8]).includes(q)):scope;
  const multi=distinct(filtered(),0)>1, hasVade=scope.some(r=>r[9]>0);
  const cols=[];
  if(multi) cols.push({k:'f',label:'Fon',type:'t',get:r=>r[0],html:r=>`<b>${esc(r[0])}</b>`});
  cols.push({k:'mk',label:'Menkul Kıymet',type:'t',get:r=>r[2],html:r=>`<b>${esc(r[2])}</b>`});
  if(hasVade){
    cols.push({k:'vl',label:'Vade',type:'t',get:r=>r[10]||'',sv:r=>r[9]||null,html:r=>esc(r[10]||'—')});
    cols.push({k:'kal',label:'Kalan gün',type:'n',get:r=>kalan(r),html:r=>{const g=kalan(r);return g==null?'—':nf(g);}});
  }
  cols.push({k:'ack',label:'Açıklama',type:'t',get:r=>r[3]||'',cls:'desc',html:r=>r[3]?esc(r[3]):'—'});
  cols.push({k:'w',label:'Ağırlık',type:'n',get:r=>r[6],html:r=>fmtW(r[6])});
  cols.push({k:'v',label:'Tahmini Değer',type:'n',get:r=>r[7],div:1e6,unit:'Mn',html:r=>money(r[7])});
  return {cols,rows:items,defSort:{k:'v',dir:'desc'},maxH:560,rowAttr:r=>`data-row="${r[11]}"`,
    after:rs=>{
      const el=$('detailSum'); if(!el) return;
      const tv=rs.reduce((s,r)=>s+r[7],0), nfn=distinct(rs,0)||1, tw=rs.reduce((s,r)=>s+r[6],0);
      el.innerHTML=rs.length?`<b>${nf(rs.length)}</b> / ${nf(scope.length)} kalem · <b>${nfn}</b> fon · toplam değer <b title="${fmtFull(tv)}">${fmtTL(tv)}</b> · ${nfn>1?'fon başına ort. ağırlık':'toplam ağırlık'} <b>${fmtW(tw/nfn)}</b>`:'Eşleşen kalem yok.';
    }};
});

function renderPage1(){
  const rows=filtered();
  const {list,nF,mv}=aggregate(rows);
  if(state.sel && !list.some(o=>o.key===state.sel)) {state.sel=null;state.dq='';gtReset('detail');}
  const totV=rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+r[7],0);
  const nT=FX.filter(f=>f.tasf==='EVET').length;
  const kp=[
    ['Fon Sayısı', nf(nF), state.funds.size? 'seçili fon' : 'tasfiye sürecindeki fonlar'],
    ['16 Eylül Portföy Büyüklüğü', nf(OZET_TASF_16EYLUL/1e9,1)+' Mr ₺', 'tasfiye sürecindeki '+nf(nT)+' fonun toplamı'],
    ...(AYAR.tasf_on_top>0?[[esc(AYAR.tasf_on_tar)+' Portföy Büyüklüğü', nf(AYAR.tasf_on_top/1e9,1)+' Mr ₺', 'tasfiye sürecindeki '+nf(nT)+' fonun toplamı']]:[]),
    [esc(OZET_GUN)+' Portföy Büyüklüğü', nf(totV/1e9,1)+' Mr ₺', state.funds.size? 'seçili '+nf(nF)+' fonun toplamı' : 'tasfiye sürecindeki '+nf(nT)+' fonun toplamı']
  ];
  $('kpis').innerHTML=kp.map(k=>`<div class="kpi"><div class="l">${k[0]}</div><div class="v">${k[1]}</div><div class="s">${k[2]}</div></div>`).join('');
  renderDonut(list,mv,nF);
  $('tableTitle').textContent = (state.group===4?'Varlık türü':'Ana varlık sınıfı')+' özeti';
  gtRender('sum');
  renderDetail();
  gtRender('tasf50');
}

function arcPath(cx,cy,r0,r1,a0,a1){
  const p=(r,a)=>[cx+r*Math.cos(a),cy+r*Math.sin(a)];
  const large=(a1-a0)>Math.PI?1:0;
  const [x0,y0]=p(r1,a0),[x1,y1]=p(r1,a1),[x2,y2]=p(r0,a1),[x3,y3]=p(r0,a0);
  return `M${x0} ${y0}A${r1} ${r1} 0 ${large} 1 ${x1} ${y1}L${x2} ${y2}A${r0} ${r0} 0 ${large} 0 ${x3} ${y3}Z`;
}
function renderDonut(list,mv,nF){
  const pos=list.map(o=>({o,val:Math.max(0,mv(o))})).filter(x=>x.val>0);
  const total=pos.reduce((s,x)=>s+x.val,0);
  $('donutTitle').textContent = (state.group===4?'Varlık türüne göre':'Ana varlık sınıfına göre')+' dağılım ('+(state.metric==='v'?'tahmini değer':'portföy ağırlığı')+')';
  let svg='', a=-Math.PI/2;
  if(pos.length===1){
    const c=colorOf[state.group].get(pos[0].o.key);
    svg=`<path class="slice ${state.sel&&state.sel!==pos[0].o.key?'dim':''}" data-key="${esc(pos[0].o.key)}" data-tip="${esc(pos[0].o.key)}|100,0%" fill="${c}" fill-rule="evenodd" d="M150 20A130 130 0 1 1 149.99 20ZM150 70A80 80 0 1 0 150.01 70Z"/>`;
  } else {
    pos.forEach(x=>{
      const ang=x.val/total*Math.PI*2, a1=a+ang;
      const c=colorOf[state.group].get(x.o.key);
      const pct=x.val/total*100;
      svg+=`<path class="slice ${state.sel&&state.sel!==x.o.key?'dim':''}" data-key="${esc(x.o.key)}" data-tip="${esc(x.o.key)}|${nf(pct,1)}%" fill="${c}" stroke="var(--card)" stroke-width="1.5" d="${arcPath(150,150,80,130,a,a1-0.0001)}"/>`;
      a=a1;
    });
  }
  $('donut').innerHTML=svg;
  $('center').innerHTML = state.metric==='v'
    ? `<div class="big">${fmtTL(list.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.v,0))}</div><div class="sm">toplam tahmini değer</div>`
    : `<div class="big">${fmtW(total)}</div><div class="sm">${nF>1?'ort. ':''}toplam ağırlık</div>`;
  const negs=list.filter(o=>mv(o)<0);
  $('donutNote').textContent = negs.length ? `Negatif değerli gruplar (${negs.map(o=>o.key).join(', ')}) grafikte gösterilmez; tabloda yer alır.${state.metric==='v'?' Ortadaki toplam, bu gruplar dahil net tutardır.':''}` : '';
}

function renderDetail(){
  const el=$('detail');
  if(!state.sel){
    el.innerHTML=`<div class="empty">📊 Bir varlık türü seçildiğinde, içindeki menkul kıymetler ve açıklamaları burada görülür.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  const c=colorOf[state.group].get(state.sel), scope=detailScope();
  el.innerHTML=`<div class="detailhead"><h2><span class="dot" style="background:${c}"></span>${esc(state.sel)} <span class="chip">${scope.length} kalem</span></h2>
    <div class="ctrls"><input type="search" id="detailSearch" placeholder="Hızlı arama…" value="${esc(state.dq)}"><button class="btn" id="clearSel">Seçimi kaldır</button></div></div>
    <div class="sumline" id="detailSum"></div>
    ${gtBox('detail')}
    <div class="note">Başlıklara tıklanarak sıralama yapılabilir; “Filtrele” ile sütunlara göre daraltılabilir. Ayrıntı için bir satıra tıklanabilir.</div>`;
  gtRender('detail');
}

/* ================= SAYFA 2: KURUM DAĞILIMLARI ================= */
function qInfo(vk){ if(!vk) return null; const y=Math.floor(vk/10000), m=Math.floor(vk/100)%100, q=Math.ceil(m/3); return {key:y*10+q,y,q}; }
function panelRows(){
  const base=scopeRows();
  return state.kSel ? base.filter(r=>r[8]===state.kSel) : base.filter(r=>r[9]>0);
}
function quarters(rows){
  const m=new Map();
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    const qi=qInfo(r[9]); const key=qi?qi.key:0;
    let o=m.get(key); if(!o){o={key,y:qi?qi.y:0,q:qi?qi.q:0,n:0,v:0,f:new Set(),mk:new Set()};m.set(key,o)}
    o.n++;o.v+=r[7];o.f.add(r[0]);o.mk.add(r[2]);
  });
  const dated=[...m.values()].filter(o=>o.key).sort((a,b)=>a.key-b.key);
  const filled=[];
  if(dated.length){
    let y=dated[0].y,q=dated[0].q; const ey=dated[dated.length-1].y,eq=dated[dated.length-1].q;
    while(y<ey||(y===ey&&q<=eq)){
      const key=y*10+q, o=m.get(key)||{key,y,q,n:0,v:0,f:new Set(),mk:new Set()};
      o.past=new Date(y,q*3,0)<REF; filled.push(o);
      q++; if(q>4){q=1;y++;}
    }
  }
  return {filled,dated,vadesiz:m.get(0)||null};
}
const qLabel=o=>o.key?`${o.y} Q${o.q}`:'Vadesiz';

function qChart(qs){
  if(!qs.length) return '<div class="empty">Bu kapsamda vade bilgisi olan kalem yok.</div>';
  const bw=50,H=200,top=26,base=152,W=qs.length*bw+10;
  const mx=Math.max(1,...qs.map(x=>Math.max(0,x.v)));
  let s=`<div class="qwrap"><svg width="${W}" height="${H}" viewBox="0 0 ${W} ${H}" style="display:block;max-width:none">`;
  qs.forEach((x,i)=>{
    const h=Math.max(0,x.v)/mx*(base-top), xx=5+i*bw, y=base-h, cx=xx+(bw-10)/2;
    const sel=state.qSel===x.key, dim=state.qSel!=null&&!sel;
    s+=`<g class="qbar ${dim?'dim':''}" data-q="${x.key}" data-tip="${x.y} Q${x.q} (${QM[x.q-1]})|${fmtTL(x.v)} · ${x.n} kalem">
      <rect x="${xx-2}" y="${top-16}" width="${bw-6}" height="${H-top+12}" fill="transparent"/>
      <rect x="${xx}" y="${y}" width="${bw-10}" height="${x.v>0?Math.max(h,2):0}" rx="5" fill="${x.past?'var(--muted)':'var(--accent)'}" opacity="${x.past?0.45:1}" ${sel?'stroke="var(--text)" stroke-width="2"':''}/>
      <text x="${cx}" y="${y-6}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="var(--text)">${x.v>0?fmtShort(x.v):''}</text>
      <text x="${cx}" y="${base+17}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="11" font-weight="600" fill="var(--text)">Q${x.q}</text>
      <text x="${cx}" y="${base+31}" text-anchor="middle" font-size="10" fill="var(--muted)">${x.y}</text></g>`;
  });
  return s+'</svg></div>';
}

/* Menkul kıymet / vade tabloları (panel + pencere ortak) */
function mkGroups(rows){
  const m=new Map();
  rows.forEach(r=>{
    const tm=isTem(r), gk=r[2]+(tm?'\u0001t':''); let o=m.get(gk); if(!o){o={mk:r[2],tem:tm,idx:r[11],best:Math.abs(r[7]),n:0,w:0,v:0,f:new Set(),vkey:r[9],vl:r[10],ens:new Set()};m.set(gk,o)}
    o.n++;o.w+=r[6];o.v+=r[7];o.f.add(r[0]);o.ens.add(ensOf(r));
    if(Math.abs(r[7])>o.best){o.best=Math.abs(r[7]);o.idx=r[11];}
  });
  return [...m.values()].map(o=>({...o,kal:kalanKey(o.vkey)}));
}
let _SZ=null; const scopeSize=rows=>{ if(!_SZ) _SZ=new Map(FX.map(f=>[f.kod,f.tut1])); return [...new Set(rows.map(r=>r[0]))].reduce((s,k)=>s+(_SZ.get(k)||0),0); };
function mkCfg(rows,nF,hl,maxH,tot){
  const groups=mkGroups(rows); const pw=v=>tot>0?v/tot*100:0;
  return {maxH:maxH||380, rows:groups, defSort:{k:'vl',dir:'asc'}, rowAttr:o=>`data-mkidx="${o.idx}"`, rowCls:o=>o.mk===hl?'hl':'',
    cols:[
      {k:'mk',label:'Menkul kıymet',type:'t',get:o=>o.mk,html:o=>`<b>${esc(o.mk)}</b>${o.tem?' <span class="tem-tag">Ters Repo Teminatı</span>':''}<div class="gray">${esc([...o.ens].join(', '))}${o.n>1?` · ${o.n} kalem`:''}</div>`},
      {k:'vl',label:'Vade',type:'t',get:o=>o.vl||'',sv:o=>o.vkey||null,html:o=>esc(o.vl||'—')},
      {k:'kal',label:'Kalan gün',type:'n',get:o=>o.kal,html:o=>o.kal==null?'—':nf(o.kal)},
      {k:'f',label:'Fon',type:'n',get:o=>o.f.size,html:o=>nf(o.f.size)},
      {k:'w',label:nF>1?'Portföydeki Payı':'Ağırlık',type:'n',get:o=>pw(o.v),html:o=>fmtW(pw(o.v))},
      {k:'v',label:'Bakiye',type:'n',get:o=>o.v,div:1e6,unit:'Mn',html:o=>money(o.v)}
    ],
    footer:rs=>{const tv=rs.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.v,0),tw=rs.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.w,0);
      return `<tr><td><b>Toplam</b></td><td></td><td></td><td></td><td class="r"><b>${fmtW(pw(tv))}</b></td><td class="r"><b>${money(tv)}</b></td></tr>`;}
  };
}

gtReg('pQ',()=>{
  const rows=panelRows(); const {dated,vadesiz}=quarters(rows);
  const tot=dated.reduce((s,o)=>s+Math.max(0,o.v),0)||1; let cum=0;
  const list=dated.map(o=>{cum+=Math.max(0,o.v);return {...o,pay:Math.max(0,o.v)/tot*100,cum:cum/tot*100};});
  if(vadesiz) list.push({...vadesiz,pay:null,cum:null});
  return {maxH:380, rows:list, defSort:{k:'q',dir:'asc'}, rowAttr:o=>`data-q="${o.key}"`, rowCls:o=>state.qSel===o.key?'sel':'',
    cols:[
      {k:'q',label:'Dönem',type:'t',get:o=>qLabel(o),sv:o=>o.key||9e9,html:o=>`<b>${esc(qLabel(o))}</b>${o.key?` <span class="gray">${QM[o.q-1]}</span>`:''}`},
      {k:'n',label:'Kalem',type:'n',get:o=>o.n,html:o=>nf(o.n)},
      {k:'f',label:'Fon',type:'n',get:o=>o.f.size,html:o=>nf(o.f.size)},
      {k:'v',label:'Vadesi gelen bakiye',type:'n',get:o=>o.v,div:1e6,unit:'Mn',html:o=>money(o.v)},
      {k:'pay',label:'Pay',type:'n',get:o=>o.pay,html:o=>o.pay==null?'—':fmtW(o.pay)},
      {k:'cum',label:'Kümülatif',type:'n',get:o=>o.cum,html:o=>o.cum==null?'—':fmtW(o.cum)}
    ],
    footer:rs=>{const tv=rs.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.v,0),tn=rs.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.n,0);
      return `<tr><td><b>Toplam</b></td><td class="r"><b>${nf(tn)}</b></td><td></td><td class="r"><b>${money(tv)}</b></td><td></td><td></td></tr>`;}
  };
});
gtReg('pMk',()=>{
  const base=scopeRows(); const nF=Math.max(1,distinct(base,0));
  let rr=panelRows();
  if(state.qSel!=null) rr=rr.filter(r=>{const q=qInfo(r[9]);return (q?q.key:0)===state.qSel;});
  return mkCfg(rr,nF,null,420,scopeSize(filtered()));
});
gtReg('pBrk',()=>{
  const rows=panelRows();
  const bm=new Map(); rows.forEach(r=>{const key=ensOf(r)+'||'+r[4];const o=bm.get(key)||{e:ensOf(r),t:r[4],n:0,v:0};o.n++;o.v+=r[7];bm.set(key,o);});
  return {maxH:380, rows:[...bm.values()], defSort:{k:'v',dir:'desc'},
    cols:[
      {k:'e',label:'Enstrüman',type:'t',get:o=>o.e,html:o=>`<b>${esc(o.e)}</b>`},
      {k:'t',label:'Varlık türü',type:'t',get:o=>o.t},
      {k:'n',label:'Kalem',type:'n',get:o=>o.n,html:o=>nf(o.n)},
      {k:'v',label:'Bakiye',type:'n',get:o=>o.v,div:1e6,unit:'Mn',html:o=>money(o.v)}
    ],
    footer:rs=>`<tr><td><b>Toplam</b></td><td></td><td class="r"><b>${nf(rs.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.n,0))}</b></td><td class="r"><b>${money(rs.reduce((s,o)=>s+o.v,0))}</b></td></tr>`
  };
});
gtReg('pFon',()=>{
  const rows=panelRows();
  const fm=new Map(); rows.forEach(r=>{const o=fm.get(r[0])||{c:r[0],a:r[1],n:0,v:0,w:0};o.n++;o.v+=r[7];o.w+=r[6];fm.set(r[0],o);});
  return {maxH:380, rows:[...fm.values()], defSort:{k:'v',dir:'desc'},
    cols:[
      {k:'c',label:'Fon',type:'t',get:o=>o.c,html:o=>`<b>${esc(o.c)}</b>`},
      {k:'a',label:'Fon adı',type:'t',get:o=>o.a,html:o=>`<span class="gray">${esc(o.a)}</span>`},
      {k:'n',label:'Kalem',type:'n',get:o=>o.n,html:o=>nf(o.n)},
      {k:'w',label:'Ağırlık',type:'n',get:o=>o.w,html:o=>fmtW(o.w)},
      {k:'v',label:'Bakiye',type:'n',get:o=>o.v,div:1e6,unit:'Mn',html:o=>money(o.v)}
    ]
  };
});

function renderPanel(){
  const rows=panelRows(), base=scopeRows();
  const tv=rows.reduce((s,r)=>s+r[7],0), totScope=base.reduce((s,r)=>s+r[7],0)||1;
  let wn=0,wd=0,w90=0,first=null,last=null;
  rows.forEach(r=>{const g=kalan(r); if(g==null||g<0) return;
    if(r[7]>0){wn+=r[7]*g;wd+=r[7];}
    if(g<=90) w90+=r[7];
    if(!first||r[9]<first[0]) first=[r[9],r[10]];
    if(!last||r[9]>last[0]) last=[r[9],r[10]];});
  const wam=wd?wn/wd:null;
  const {filled}=quarters(rows);
  const name=state.kSel||'Tüm vadeli kalemler';
  const sub=state.kSel?(kTip.get(state.kSel)||''):'Soldan bir kurum seçildiğinde ayrıntısı görülür';
  if(state.kSel && !rows.length){
    $('panel').innerHTML=`<div class="p2head"><div><h2>${esc(name)}</h2><div class="gray">${esc(sub)}</div></div><button class="btn" id="kClear">Seçimi kaldır</button></div>
      <div class="empty">Bu kurumun, seçili fonlarda (ya da teminat ayarında) kalemi yok.</div>`;
    return;
  }
  const kp=[
    ['Toplam bakiye',fmtTL(tv),`${nf(rows.length)} kalem · ${nf(distinct(rows,0))} fon`+(()=>{const tt=rows.filter(isTem).reduce((s,r)=>s+r[7],0);return tt?` · ${fmtTL(tt)} ters repo teminatı`:'';})()],
    ['Ağırlıklı ort. vade',wam==null?'—':nf(wam,0)+' gün',wam==null?'vadeli kalem yok':'≈ '+nf(wam/30.4,1)+' ay'],
    ['90 gün içinde vadesi gelen',fmtTL(w90),tv>0?fmtW(w90/tv*100)+' · toplamın payı':''],
    ['Vade aralığı',first?`${first[1]} → ${last[1]}`:'—','en yakın → en uzak']
  ];
  const chipQ = state.qSel!=null ? `<span class="chip">Dönem: ${state.qSel? Math.floor(state.qSel/10)+' Q'+(state.qSel%10):'Vadesiz'}<button class="xbtn" data-q="${state.qSel}" title="Dönem filtresini kaldır">✕</button></span>` : '';
  $('panel').innerHTML=`
    <div class="p2head"><div><h2>${esc(name)}</h2><div class="gray">${esc(sub)}</div></div>
      ${state.kSel?'<button class="btn" id="kClear">Seçimi kaldır</button>':''}</div>
    ${state.kSel&&kDescOf(state.kSel)?`<div class="kdesc">${esc(kDescOf(state.kSel))}</div>
